# CVE Code and Patch Enrichment Status

This file tracks the staged enrichment of vulnerability reports under `cve/`. It distinguishes advisory-designated fix commits from code changes that are merely referenced by an advisory or verified in a fixed tree.

## Coverage Boundary

The folder inventory contains 660 `CVE-*.md` reports and 62 `GHSA-*.md` reports (722 vulnerability reports), plus `README.md` and this status file. This log covers the 10 reports in Batch 1, `CVE-2026-26327`, and the reports enumerated in the deep-analysis rework sets below. At this checkpoint, **663/722 vulnerability records are covered and 59 remain unaudited**. Full folder coverage requires additional batches. One dual-identifier filename (`CVE-2026-35620__GHSA-vqvg-86cc-cg83.md`) is counted as two vulnerability records but one Markdown file. Historical set number `141` was not used; the coverage checkpoints, report identifiers, and current counts are authoritative.

## Environment Note

Direct `git clone` access to GitHub failed in earlier batches. Public GitHub commit pages were used to verify those diffs; sets 7 through 9 additionally verified public `.patch`, raw source, GitHub commit API, and repository advisory API responses. Sets 10 and 11 verified public vendor advisory pages, `.patch` files, and parent/final raw source through the browser; sets 12 through 15 used public advisory pages, commit patches/API, and parent/fixed raw source where available. Direct terminal GitHub requests failed during this run with an SSL connection error; anonymous GitHub REST API requests were rate-limited. Browser-accessible advisory pages, commit patches, and raw source were used instead. Upstream test code was inspected but not executed locally.
